# Shared-file claims (append-only)

Format: `<UTC time> <agent/ws> CLAIM|RELEASE <path relative to the integration tree>`

Rules:
- Before you edit a shared file, check that its latest line is a RELEASE (or that it has no line at all).
- Release the claim as soon as your edit is done.
- The integrator alone edits TESTING-GAP-REGISTER.md.
